# Skills · T · part 10

893 entries, teach-and-check to team-motivation-ops. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **teach-and-check**: Teach a kid a new idea as you build their game or app, then check they understood it before unlocking the next thing they want — earning Brain Points. Use this EVERY time you're about to add something to a kid's creation that introduces a genuinely-new concept (a database/"memory box", a loop, a score, saving, a type-in box, a button, putting it online, etc.). The learn-as-you-build gate, for games and apps alike. Reads the kid's age and the grown-up's strictness dial from the Player Card (see about-me) and writes learned concepts to it. ([jackneil/claude-jacked-kids](https://github.com/jackneil/claude-jacked-kids/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude-jacked-kids/skills/teach-and-check))
- **teach-and-explain**: Create explanations, tutoring sequences, study material, exam support, worked derivations, active-recall prompts, and accessible learning content. Use when the user wants to understand, practise, remember, or be coached rather than merely receive a finished artifact. Adapt depth, notation, pacing, and cognitive load from supplied learning context without reducing conceptual rigor. ([optimelv/contextual-writing-skill](https://github.com/optimelv/contextual-writing-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/teach-and-explain))
- **teach-anything**: Planejar e conduzir aulas sobre qualquer assunto, seguindo um roteiro didático em 6 passos, respeitando estritamente o escopo de tópicos definido na requisição, verificando a atualidade dos dados antes de escrever, gerando arquivos Markdown e uma apresentação reveal.js de suporte, e registrando feedback ao final para melhorar a skill a cada iteração. ([renanalencar/nan-skills](https://github.com/renanalencar/nan-skills/tree/HEAD/teach-anything))
- **teach-architecture**: Explain how one or more completed features fit into the wider application and system architecture. ([rikilamadrid/pathfinder](https://github.com/rikilamadrid/pathfinder/tree/HEAD/skills/teach-architecture) · ★1 · 2 copies)

([tmusser/ai-engineering-skills](https://github.com/tmusser/ai-engineering-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/teach-back) · ★4)
- **teach-back**: Optional capstone after project completion: write a Socratic-style blog post on a formerly-shaky topic, compare against the masters, decide whether to publish ([AnjanJ/bodhikit](https://github.com/AnjanJ/bodhikit/tree/HEAD/skills/teach-back) · ★2)
- **teach-back**: Quiz the user on a document, one question at a time, until they can explain it to someone else — definitions, numbers, evidence, background, scope — and log the document's own gaps separately. For material that makes causal claims or rests on hypotheses (incident reports, post-mortems, proposals), also runs armor mode: sorts fact from hypothesis, checks the cause-and-effect chain, cross-examines it, and hands over what to assert, hedge and leave out. Use when the user wants to understand material rather than have it summarised, is preparing to explain, report or present a document, or says 「理… ([atman-33/workhub](https://github.com/atman-33/workhub/tree/HEAD/plugins/thinking/skills/teach-back) · ★2)
- **teach-back**: Helps users teach what they built to others. Generates teaching guides, workshop outlines, and coaches the user on simplifying their explanations. Use when someone wants to train teammates or share knowledge. ([ChewbaccaRoars/pathfinder-skills](https://github.com/ChewbaccaRoars/pathfinder-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/teach-back) · ★2)
- **teach-back**: Critique a user's own explanation of a concept via an assigned expert persona, surface gaps, and work through them until resolved. Use when the user wants to explain something back to check their understanding. Trigger only with /teach-back. ([behnamazimi/skills](https://github.com/behnamazimi/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/teach-back) · ★1)
- **teach-back**: Explain a concept back to your sensei to test your mental model ([jreynolds-dev/sensei](https://github.com/jreynolds-dev/sensei/tree/HEAD/skills/teach-back) · ★1)
- **teach-back**: 計画・設計・重要な依頼を実装前にユーザー自身の言葉で説明してもらい、目的・構造・判断・リスク・検証・復旧の理解を採点し、不足部分だけ再学習する。Use when the user explicitly asks for teach-back, Teach Back, 理解確認, 言い換え採点, 「要するにこうで合ってる？」, or accepts the proposal shown after grill-me completes a material plan, especially before multi-agent implementation, auth/DB/PII changes, external release, or customer delivery. Do not use for minor reversible edits. ([tomosuke-chiba/kou-skills](https://github.com/tomosuke-chiba/kou-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/goal-align/skills/teach-back) · ★1)
- **teach-back**: La prueba definitiva de la comprensión: ser capaz de enseñar lo aprendido de forma sencilla. ([jpviola/eduSkills](https://github.com/jpviola/eduSkills/tree/HEAD/skills/lbd/teach-back))
- **teach-back**: Forces real understanding of a concept by explaining it in the plainest possible language — as if to a 10-year-old — so that every place the explanation stumbles exposes a genuine gap in understanding (the Feynman technique). Use this skill when the user is learning something new and wants to check whether they actually get it: phrases like "explain like I'm 5", "ELI5", "do I actually understand this", "help me learn X", "I'm studying X", "quiz my understanding", "make sure this sticks", or when they ask you to explain a concept simply or to let them teach it back to you and probe the holes. ([opelpleple/meta-skills](https://github.com/opelpleple/meta-skills/tree/HEAD/teach-back))
- **teach-back**: User explains a concept back to prove mastery. The most demanding and most effective technique. Use when the user explicitly asks to review or when testing deep understanding. ([VedSoni-dev/cognition-skills](https://github.com/VedSoni-dev/cognition-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/learn/techniques/teach-back))
- **Teach-Back Script Writer**: This skill drafts teach-back prompts that check whether a patient or learner understood a health instruction, in the teach-back method: the open question that asks the person to restate the instruction, the follow-up for gaps, and the framing that avoids a test-like tone. It works from an instruction the user provides; it does not give health advice, interpret anyone's situation, or decide what a person should be told. ([salikkhann/openmedskills](https://github.com/salikkhann/openmedskills/tree/HEAD/skills/patient-communication/teach-back-script-writer) · ★1)
